# OddsIntel — Master Priority Queue

> **✅ COOLBET-CDP-JWT-EXTRACT Done 2026-06-12** — Eliminates the JWT-expiry-triggers-SMS loop that caused the 2026-06-11 overnight SMS storm. The operator's CDP-Chrome session (`Chrome-CDP-OddsIntel` profile, `--remote-debugging-port=9222`) auto-renews the JWT every ~20 min via Coolbet's frontend `/s/auth/renew-token` call. New `workers/automation/coolbet_browser_sync.extract_jwt_from_cdp()` connects via **raw CDP over websockets** (not Playwright — Playwright's page enumeration crashes with "Frame was detached" on the operator's daily-driver Chrome with 60+ active targets) and reads `localStorage['cbauth']` (plus a defensive scan of all keys for JWT-shaped values). `coolbet_session._login()` calls `_try_cdp_jwt()` BEFORE the SMS-blocked raise — so an expired JWT silently self-heals from the open browser. New `--refresh-jwt` CLI for explicit operator-triggered refresh, with `--resume-placement` to also clear the `placement_paused` kill switch in one step. Also **fixed a real safety bug**: `place_all_bets(execute=True)` now checks `is_placement_paused()` (previously only the signaler path checked it — the Mac daemon was placing regardless of the kill switch; only JWT-expired exception incidentally stopped placement during the 2026-06-12 incident). New doc `COOLBET_ARCHITECTURE.md` at repo root documents the full Mac daemon + CDP-Chrome + FlareSolverr + Railway split. Verified live: JWT extracted with TTL=1601s, persisted to `coolbet_session_state.jwt_current` + `jwt_exp_at`, daemon tick ran clean (qualified=4, placed=0 due to paused, errors=0). Smoke: COOLBET-CDP-JWT-EXTRACT, COOLBET-PLACEMENT-PAUSED-KILL-SWITCH.

> ⬜ **COOLBET-CDP-FETCH-RAW-MIGRATE** — `workers/automation/coolbet_browser_sync.fetch_pending_bets_via_cdp()` still uses Playwright/patchright and hits "Frame was detached" on the operator's busy daily-driver Chrome. Symptom: daemon log shows `CDP connect failed at http://localhost:9222 — is Chrome running with --remote-debugging-port=9222?` even though Chrome IS running. Migrate to the same raw-CDP-websocket pattern that `extract_jwt_from_cdp` now uses. Currently degrades gracefully (falls back to user_placed_at-only dedup) but means manual bets you place yourself could be re-attempted by the daemon if you don't tap the Telegram ✅ button.

> ⬜ **COOLBET-DEAD-FILE-CLEANUP** — Now that the CDP-JWT path is live, several files are confirmed dead. Low-risk candidates: `scripts/coolbet_refresh_jwt.py`, `scripts/coolbet_refresh_debug.py`, `scripts/coolbet_browser_setup.py`, `scripts/_daemon_handlers.py`, `scripts/place_one_real_bet.py`, `scripts/coolbet/session_heartbeat.py` (superseded by `health_ping.py`), `scripts/coolbet/flaresolverr_login_test.py`, `scripts/coolbet/inspect_2fa_endpoint.py`. > **✅ COOLBET-SIGNALER-A Done 2026-06-12** — Replaces Railway-side auto-placer with a pure Telegram signal flow after the overnight 100+ SMS spam incident exposed the fragility of the Imperva→FS-Chrome→JWT→SMS-2FA chain. The signaler does ZERO Coolbet API calls in the hot path: reads `simulated_bets` for qualified picks (same `_min_edge_for()` floors as the placer — 1X2 ≥10%, O/U ≥3%, AH ≥5%, BTTS ≥10%, combo ≥10%, DC retired), DISTINCT ON (match,market,selection) to collapse multi-bot picks, sends one Telegram per bet, marks ALL sibling rows `signaled_at = NOW()` to prevent re-fire next pipeline run. Mig 246 adds `simulated_bets.signaled_at`; mig 247 adds `matches.coolbet_match_id` populated lazily by an anon Coolbet search so signals carry direct match-page deep links (e.g. `https://www.coolbet.com/et/sport/match/5616595`). `betting_pipeline._run_coolbet_record` → `_run_coolbet_signal`; `place_all_bets(record=True)` no longer called from the pipeline. Validated end-to-end at 07:36 UTC: 8 qualified picks signaled cleanly, 4 of 7 matches got resolved deep links (3 lower-tier matches not listed on Coolbet → fall back to sports landing page). Telegram operator commands (`/status /pause /resume /today /help`) stripped from the webhook — they pointed at the now-defunct session-state observability. Smoke: COOLBET-SIGNALER-A. **Next: option B — Mac-at-home daemon for auto-placement from a residential IP, signaler remains as safety net.**

> **✅ COOLBET-NO-AUTO-LOGIN Done 2026-06-12** — Emergency same-morning fix after the overnight SMS spam. `CoolbetSession()` refuses to call `/s/auth/login` by default; only the enrollment script (`scripts/coolbet/flaresolverr_login_enroll.py`) sets `COOLBET_ALLOW_API_LOGIN=true` to opt in. All other contexts — heartbeat, placer, scanner — read the DB JWT and fail fast on expiry, surfacing a clean "re-enroll" instruction instead of triggering SMS 2FA every 5 min. Belt-and-suspenders: `placement_paused=TRUE` set in DB at the same time. Smoke: COOLBET-NO-AUTO-LOGIN. Largely superseded by COOLBET-SIGNALER-A but kept as defense-in-depth.

> **✅ WC-OVERNIGHT-COVERAGE Done 2026-06-12** — Closed the overnight betting dead zone that caused us to miss bets on WC matches kicking off between 22:35 UTC and 07:05 UTC. **Trigger**: Korea 2-1 Czech (02:00 UTC kickoff) had `national_team_v1_lineup` predictions ready at 01:39 UTC and 30 bookmakers of odds in the DB, but zero bots fired — no betting refresh runs in that window. Mexico-SA the night before (19:00 UTC) caught `bot_high_alignment` only because it ran at 18:36 UTC, inside the daytime window. **Fix**: two cron edits in `workers/scheduler.py` — `job_odds_refresh` loop expanded from `range(7, 23)` to `range(0, 24)` (the 20:00 carve-out for pre-KO mark_closing stays), and `job_betting_refresh_wrapper` expanded from `hour="7-22"` to `hour="*"`. The 22:00 `job_odds_tomorrow` opening-line-capture entry is unchanged (still anchors `overnight_line_move` MFV). `_current_cohort()` already maps all 24 hours (morning <11, midday <15, else pre_ko) — no crash on overnight runs. Coolbet odds snapshot + shadow runs remain windowed (Coolbet session is more fragile, shadow data quality only matters for daytime cohort comparison). Smoke `WC-OVERNIGHT-COVERAGE` pins the 24-hour loop, the `hour="*"` cron, and the preserved 20:00 carve-out. Net effect: ~16 extra `run_odds()` AF calls/day + ~18 extra `run_betting()` DB-only ticks/day, well within AF Ultra rate limit. WORKFLOWS.md updated.

> **✅ COOLBET-JWT-DB-BACKED Done 2026-06-12** — Eliminates the env-var sync that kept silently breaking Coolbet on Railway. **Problem**: Imperva 403's `/s/auth/login` from cloud datacenter IPs (Railway) but accepts it from residential IPs (local). Before today, Railway's `COOLBET_MANUAL_JWT` env var was set once via the dashboard, went stale every ~30 min, and operator had to paste a fresh JWT every time something broke. **Fix**: `coolbet_session_state.jwt_current` (mig 245) is now the canonical store. Three persistence hooks: (1) **enrollment** — `scripts/coolbet/flaresolverr_login_enroll.py._persist_jwt()` writes to DB on successful SMS verify, tagged `set_by="local_enroll"`; (2) **adopt** — `CoolbetSession._adopt_manual_jwt()` writes after any manual-JWT bootstrap, tagged `set_by="adopt_manual_jwt"`; (3) **api_login + renew-token** — both write after success. `CoolbetSession.__init__()` reads from DB via `read_persisted_jwt()` and picks the freshest valid candidate vs env via `_pick_freshest_jwt(env, db)`. The 403 error message no longer references stale `COOLBET_IMPERVA_COOKIES` — points operator at the enrollment script. **RLS tightened**: dropped the open-anon SELECT policy on `coolbet_session_state` (JWT is sensitive); created `coolbet_session_state_public` view that exposes everything except `jwt_current` for any non-service-role read path. **Verified end-to-end**: `env -u COOLBET_MANUAL_JWT python3 -c 'CoolbetSession(); keep_alive()'` succeeds — process bootstraps from DB row with no env var present. Heartbeat ✓ ok, TTL preserved, observability row updated. Tomorrow Railway picks this up on its next cron tick; operator never pastes a JWT again unless the device-trust cookie expires (months). Smoke: COOLBET-JWT-DB-BACKED.

> **✅ CS2-LEETIFY-CEILING Done 2026-06-11** — Full Leetify crawl completed (5.3h, 2,171 API calls of 10,000 budget — terminated because the recent_teammates graph fully closed). **Final state**: cs2_player_id_bridge 23 → **1,138** (49×); cs2_leetify_player_match_stats 1,660 → **75,288** rows (45×); **HLTV-bridged matches 100 → 5,077 (50×)**; densified-10 (all 10 players) 685 → 2,469 (3.6×). Re-ran v20-leetify sneak peek at every coverage level — definitive PROMOTE NO at 15.7% eval coverage on 5,077 bridged matches: leetify_rating Δ=0.0000, trade_success Δ=0.0000, multi_kill Δ=+0.0001, **preaim Δ=−0.0092 (actively destructive)**, opening Δ=0.0000, ALL stacked Δ=−0.0093. Critical finding: the "Leetify is orthogonal to v8" claim from initial probe (r=−0.272 at 3% coverage) **fully unwound at scale** — correlation between logit_saved_prob and leetify_rating_diff went −0.272 → −0.118 → −0.074 → **+0.075** as sample grew. Mechanics-level demo stats turn out to be correlated WITH v8's saved_prob, not independent of it. **v8 is the ceiling on public CS2 data.** Today's full test battery: 13 candidate feature classes, 50+ feature blocks (HLTV veto / sides / regions / map pool / recent-veto / pistols / ftu / starting-side / LAN / schedule-density / stacked-HLTV / Leetify-5-features × 5 coverage levels), **zero blocks** lift v8 AUC by ≥+0.002. Only untested avenue remaining: matchup-level features via Liquipedia role tags (#11) — could expose role-vs-role signal that team-avg aggregation washes out. Otherwise the play is **operational/sizing-side**: get Railway scanner cron healthy, optimize bot edge threshold + Kelly fraction + CLV monitoring on a now-mature production stack (28K HLTV matches, 84.5% bo3gg↔HLTV bridge, 75K Leetify rows ready for future re-use). Sneak peeks v18/v19/v20 + bridge + scanner-loop + Pinnacle fuzzy match all shipped and persist for re-evaluation when meaningful new data sources surface.

> **✅ CS2-ODDS-FLOW-FIX Done 2026-06-11** — Fixed the "bot fires 0 picks despite full v8 model coverage" issue surfaced in `/admin/cs2`. Two root causes diagnosed: (1) the Railway `cs2_scanner` cron stopped firing — no `cs2_scanner` entries in `cs2_scraper_state` for last 24h, so `bookie_odds1/2` (sourced from bo3.gg's API `coeff` field at `cs2_elo_scanner.py:668-669`) never refreshed and bot had nothing to compute edge against. Manual scanner run immediately populated **16/38** matches with bookie_odds. (2) Pinnacle scanner geo-blocked from local (`guest.api.arcadia.pinnacle.com` connection timeout) — works from Railway, but its `normalize_team()` was too weak (lowercase + strip suffixes; no alias map or fuzzy fallback), so at 11:00 UTC run it matched 0/30 fixtures. **Two fixes shipped**: (a) `scripts/esports/run_cs2_scanner_loop.sh` — local stopgap orchestrator that runs `cs2_elo_scanner.py --record` + `cs2_v8_predict.py --record` + `cs2_bot.py --record` every 30 min so odds + predictions + picks stay fresh while Railway is being diagnosed. (b) `cs2_pinnacle_scanner.py` `normalize_team()` rewritten to mirror `cs2_match_id_bridge_populate.py`'s 3-stage matcher: NFD-deaccent + lowercase → 44-entry alias map (FaZe Clan↔FaZe, Spirit↔Team Spirit, 1win Team↔1win, NaVi↔Natus Vincere, plus 16 Pinnacle-specific like Virtus.pro↔VP, NaVi Jr variants, Betclic Apogee↔Apogee, Monte Gen↔Monte) → strip suffix words (esports.net, e-sports, esports, esport, gaming, academy, clan, club, team, pro, fe) → alias-map retry → `rapidfuzz.token_set_ratio` ≥70 bidirectional pair score → match. New `match_pinnacle_to_db()` helper at `cs2_pinnacle_scanner.py:259-323` with `_exact_pair`/`_norm_pair`/`_fuzzy_pair_score` sub-helpers. Also fixed BO-format safety: previous code could match against child matchups (map-handicap, map-totals) since `matchups[:limit]` was sliced BEFORE filtering — new `_is_series_matchup()` (parentId IS NULL, type='matchup', 2 participants) runs first. New `--dry-run` flag dumps up to 15 unmatched Pinnacle matchups for alias-map curation. **Investigation also clarified**: HLTV match-page odds idea WON'T work — odds are rendered by a 3rd-party JS partner widget after page load; only "betting"-related class in the static HTML is `matchpage-after-betting-desktop-mobile-new` (layout, not content). The 20 "Pinnacle" hits in HLTV HTML are from a tournament named "Yuqilin Pinnacle of Battle Season 3" in past-matches data. Smoke `CS2-PINNACLE-SCANNER-FUZZY` pins rapidfuzz import, difflib fallback, alias map keys, the 3-stage matcher functions, ±4h time-window filter, `--dry-run` mode, and the `_is_series_matchup`/period=0 BO safety — runs 6 behavioural matcher cases without hitting the live API. **Operational status**: scanner-loop running locally (PID 44472). First pass populated 14/36 upcoming with bookie_odds; bot now has comparison prices and should fire picks when v8's estimate ≥5% above bookie. Pinnacle scanner Railway-only — fix is shipped, deploys on next Railway push.

> **✅ CS2-TIER-1 + LEETIFY-INFRA Done 2026-06-11** — Completed Tier 1 of the CS2 data-sources roadmap and built Leetify ingestion infrastructure (Tier 2 marquee). **Tier 1 results**: (1) **v18-pistols** — mig 239 + `cs2_hltv_team_pistols_scraper.py` (1,144 rows across 85 teams / 4 quarterly periods, scraped from bulk `/stats/teams/pistols?maps=de_X&side=...` page since per-team URL returns 404). 3 features tested on v8: `team1_ct_pistol_diff_thismap`, `team1_t_pistol_diff_thismap`, `pistol_per_map_avg_diff`. Best delta +0.0000, PROMOTE: no on all. Coverage 12.8-15.4% — per-map pistol IS orthogonal to v8's aggregate `pistol_diff` (Pearson r = −0.092) but coverage too thin to surface lift. (2) **v19-ftu** — mig 240 + `cs2_hltv_team_ftu_scraper.py` (1,341 rows / 168 teams / 4 periods × 3 sides). **Important discovery**: HLTV's `/stats/teams/ftu` page does NOT publish per-grenade throw counts as the spec assumed — it shows 10 columns where ADR is utility-damage-per-round and FA is flash-assists-per-round, both renamed via the page's "Firepower/Teamwork/Utility" header convention. Built `util_dmg_diff`, `flash_efficiency_diff`, `nade_economy_diff`, `traded_diff`. All PROMOTE: no, best +0.0001. **Tier 1 verdict: free HLTV scrapes contribute nothing on top of v8.** **Leetify infra (Tier 2)**: (3) **mig 241** + `cs2_leetify_scraper.py` + `cs2_sneak_peek_v20_leetify.py`. Two new tables: `cs2_player_id_bridge` (steam64↔hltv_player_id) and `cs2_leetify_player_match_stats` (35 first-class columns + raw_stats JSONB for all 66 Leetify fields). Scraper handles seed-list bootstrap → /v3/profile fetch → /v3/profile/matches walk → /v2/matches/{id} densify (since /profile/matches only returns the seed player's row, not all 10 — discovered during bootstrap), with 1 req/s pacing + 429 backoff. Bootstrap of 25 KNOWN_SEEDS yielded 1,660 player-match rows / 580 matches / 100 HLTV-bridged / 216 organically-discovered new steam64s. Eval coverage only 1.6% so AUC didn't move (all blocks +0.0000 to +0.0003). **CRITICAL finding for next iteration**: `corr(logit_saved_prob, leetify_rating_diff) = −0.088` on covered rows — **Leetify is mostly orthogonal to v8's prediction** (unlike every HLTV-detail feature we tested today). The lift potential is real; coverage is the binding constraint. Next: broaden bootstrap by reseeding from the 216 organically-discovered steam64s + their `recent_teammates` graph; could 10-30× ingestion. Smoke tests: `CS2-HLTV-TEAM-PISTOLS-SCRAPER`, `CS2-SNEAK-V18-PISTOLS`, `CS2-HLTV-TEAM-FTU-SCRAPER`, `CS2-SNEAK-V19-FTU`, `CS2-LEETIFY-SCRAPER`, `CS2-SNEAK-V20-LEETIFY` all green.

> **✅ CS2-TIER-0-CLEAR + MATCH-ID-BRIDGE Done 2026-06-11** — Closing chapter on the HLTV-detail feature space against v8 production. (1) **`cs2_match_id_bridge` (mig 238)** — durable bo3gg↔HLTV lookup replacing exact-string joins that capped coverage at 0.8%. Populator `scripts/esports/cs2_match_id_bridge_populate.py` matches `cs2_results` rows to `cs2_hltv_matches` via exact name → normalised name (FaZe Clan↔FaZe, Spirit↔Team Spirit, Team-suffix strip, manual alias map) → rapidfuzz token_set_ratio fallback. **7,831 / 9,266 bridged = 84.5%** (2,714 exact / 5,111 norm_team / 6 fuzzy / 1,435 truly absent from HLTV — mostly tier-3 obscure rosters). HLTV backfill stored ~90% of matches at "fetch time" placeholder timestamps so populator detects placeholder buckets (≥100 matches sharing one timestamp) and falls back to greedy chronological name-pair assignment with confidence -1 notch. Bridge is rerun-safe via `ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE`. Smoke `CS2-MATCH-ID-BRIDGE` pins schema + helpers + `normalise_team` unit checks. (2) **Re-ran v10/v13/v14 sneak peeks with bridge join + PIT-strict decider_winrate** (decider was leaking eval-row outcome via all-time `per_team_map_winrate` aggregate — collapsed yesterday's reported +0.0739 v10 lift to nothing). Coverage jumped 25-35× (v10 0.8%→19.2%, v13 2.4%→62.7%, v14 2.4%→85.8%). All blocks **PROMOTE: no**: v10-pit −0.0099, v10-permaban −0.0043, v13 +0.0001, v14 −0.0034. (3) **Stacked variant `v15-stack-all`** = v10-pit + v13 + v14 simultaneously on v8 = −0.0137 AUC; sum-of-individuals = −0.0150 so they're additive (no constructive interaction). (4) **v16 map-pool features** (team_pool_familiarity_diff + pool_age_diff + new_map_played_diff) — all PROMOTE: no, deltas in [−0.0027, +0.0002]. v8's `tm_diff`/`form_diff` absorb the signal. (5) **v17 recent-veto features** (recent_permaban_concentration + recent_decider_winrate_recent30 + recent_perm_overlap, last-30-sessions / 90-day window) — all PROMOTE: no, best +0.0016 (below +0.002 threshold). Hypothesis was directionally right (teams change top-3 permabans 85% of the time over 30-day shifts) but veto data is sparse pre-Apr-2026 so eval coverage only 1.7%. **Conclusion**: with PIT-correct measurement and proper coverage, **the HLTV match-detail feature space is empty on top of v8** — v8's `form_diff/h2h_diff/kd_diff/rest_diff/rank_diff/pistol_diff/tier_*/bo_centered/saved_prob` stack already absorbs whatever marginal signal v10/v13/v14/v16/v17 contribute. Time to pivot to **independent signal sources**: Leetify ingestion (Tier 2, +0.010-0.018 est, marquee project) and Liquipedia roles (Tier 3). Sneak peeks `v15_stack.py`, `v16_mappool.py`, `v17_recent_veto.py` shipped + smoke-tested for future re-runs once data depth grows further.

> **✅ CS2-HLTV-NATIVE-BACKTEST + RESULTS-BACKFILL Done 2026-06-10** — Two-pronged response to the data-window mismatch found in CS2-FEATURE-COVERAGE-AUDIT (below). **Route 1 (backfill)**: walker investigation revealed earlier "+98 of 6798 attempted" claim was wrong; `/results?offset=N` paginates densely all the way to ~120,000 matches. Live probe at offset=12000 returned 110 matches, 101 of which were not in our queue. New `scripts/esports/run_match_details_loop.sh` orchestrator wraps the queue walker (`--queue --pages 1200`) and the match-details processor in a single local job — processor auto-restarts whenever walker pushes new IDs, exits cleanly when both walker is gone and queue is empty for 3 consecutive checks. Currently running locally (foreground via `tee dev/active/match_details_local.log`) — full backfill ETA ~3 days at ~30 matches/min. **Route 2 (HLTV-native eval harness)**: new `scripts/esports/cs2_hltv_native_backtest.py` evaluates v10/v13/v14 against `cs2_hltv_matches.winner_name` ground truth instead of `cs2_results`, since the HLTV-detail features and HLTV-result outcomes share the same window. Coverage jumped 0.6% → **91.9% (v10)**, 2.1% → **93.5% (v13)**, 1.7% → **52.9% (v14)**. Results on cutoff 2026-05-01 (n_train=694, n_eval=8579): **v10-veto = +0.0739 AUC vs baseline (0.6075 → 0.6815, PROMOTE candidate)**, v13-side = −0.0207 (clear no), v14-region = +0.0004 (noise — eval window dominated by ONLINE June 2026 matches). **Important caveat on v10**: `decider_winrate_diff` currently uses all-time per_team_map_winrate aggregate, which lets the current match's own outcome feed its own feature. True PIT-correct rebuild needed before treating +0.074 as a real lift — likely shrinks to +0.02-0.04. Persisted to `cs2_model_backtest_history` as `hltv-native-baseline` + `hltv-native-v10` + `hltv-native-v13` + `hltv-native-v14`. Smoke `CS2-HLTV-NATIVE-BACKTEST` pins the harness + all three feature-block names + persistence pattern.

> **✅ CS2-FEATURE-COVERAGE-AUDIT Done 2026-06-10** — Drained the match-details queue (9,273 matches → `cs2_hltv_matches` + `cs2_hltv_match_veto` + `cs2_hltv_match_maps`) and immediately tested three new sneak-peek features that all depend on per-match HLTV detail: v10-veto (permaban_match_diff + decider_winrate_diff + forced_off_permaban), v13-starting-side (CT-start win-rate diff + bias-aligned-diff for Nuke/Anubis/Overpass), v14-lan-region (is_lan_event + lan_winrate_diff + region_advantage_diff). **All three landed PROMOTE: no, but not for the reason expected.** Coverage on each: v10 0.6%, v13 2.1%, v14 1.7%. Root cause discovered in v13/v14 logs: `cs2_predictions ⨝ cs2_results` (the backtest universe via bo3gg settlement) is dense Jun 2025 → Mar 2026, but `cs2_hltv_matches` only became dense from **Apr 2026** (Sep 2025–Mar 2026: 17-27 rows/month; April 2026: 578; May: 604; June: 7,975 after today's drain). The two windows barely overlap, so HLTV-detail-derived features evaluate against mostly-NULL columns and the logistic learns ~zero coefficient. **Implications**: v10/v13/v14 are not bad ideas — they cannot be evaluated yet. Re-run requires either (a) accumulating ≥800 finished matches with `win_prob1` post-2026-04-01 (~1-2 months from now at current cadence), OR (b) pivoting to a data source with independent coverage. Path forward documented: **Leetify API** (probed in same session) cross-references HLTV matches via `data_source: "hltv"` + `data_source_match_id`, exposes 66 per-match per-player fields including trade_kill_percentage / multi-kills / utility / preaim / opening — i.e. all the demo-derived signals v11 was supposed to compute. Three sneak-peek scripts (`cs2_sneak_peek_v10_veto.py`, `_v13_starting_side.py`, `_v14_lan_region.py`) committed for future re-runs; smoke tests CS2-SNEAK-V11-SCHEDULE / V13-STARTING-SIDE / V14-LAN-REGION pin their existence + feature shape so re-runs don't drift. Also documented incidentally: v11-schedule-density (24h/72h B2B fatigue) does NOT lift AUC (delta −0.0013) — v8's existing `rest_diff` already absorbs the fatigue signal. PROMOTE: no, dropped from roadmap.

> **✅ ANON-AUTH-PHASE-4 Done 2026-06-10** — Hardening layer for anonymous Supabase users. Three deliverables: (1) **Cloudflare Turnstile invisible captcha** wired into `ensureAnonUser` via new `src/lib/turnstile.ts`. Loads `challenges.cloudflare.com/turnstile/v0/api.js` lazily, renders an off-screen widget per call, returns single-use token within 10s budget, falls back to `null` on any failure (script load error / timeout / expired). Token passed to `supabase.auth.signInAnonymously({options: {captchaToken}})`. **Verified enforcement** via direct API probe: Supabase returns `400 captcha_failed` when no token is provided. (2) **90-day prune cron** at `workers/jobs/prune_anon_users.py`, scheduled Sun 02:00 UTC. Deletes `auth.users WHERE is_anonymous=TRUE AND last_sign_in_at < NOW() - INTERVAL '90 days'`; cascades to profiles + favorites + picks. Hard cap of 10k rows per run as a config-error safety guard. (3) **Ops dashboard anon metrics** (mig 237) — adds `anon_users_total`, `anon_users_today`, `anon_users_engaged_7d`, `anon_upgrades_7d` columns to `ops_snapshots`. `write_ops_snapshot` populates them from `auth.users` + activity tables. Also **fixes** `total_users` / `pro_users` / `elite_users` / `new_signups_today` to count only `email IS NOT NULL` rows — earlier they conflated anon test artifacts with real signups (showed "47 users" when the truth was "38 real + 9 anon fly-bys"). User manually toggled Cloudflare Turnstile widget + Supabase Auth → Attack Protection captcha; `NEXT_PUBLIC_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY` set in Vercel envs. **WAF rate-limit rule** on `/auth/v1/signup` still pending (user-side Cloudflare dashboard, optional defense in depth).

> **✅ ANON-AUTH-PHASE-3 Done 2026-06-10** — In-place upgrade UX for anonymous users. `<UpgradeModal />` component supports three paths, all preserving `user.id` (so favorites + tracker picks survive the upgrade automatically): (1) Google + Discord OAuth via `supabase.auth.linkIdentity({provider})` — required enabling "Allow manual linking" in Supabase Auth → Providers; (2) Email + password via `supabase.auth.updateUser({email, password})`; (3) Merge-conflict path when email already belongs to a different real account — surfaces "sign in to existing (current favorites lost)" with explicit data-loss warning. Modal mounted in root layout via `<UpgradeModalMount />`; `useAuth().openUpgradeModal(trigger)` exposed to any descendant. Three trigger paths wired: (a) After the **3rd favorite** added by an anon user, MatchFavoriteButton opens modal 350ms after star fills (so animation lands first); (b) **`/pricing` Pro upgrade click** by anon user — API returns 403 `anonymous_upgrade_required`, PricingCards catches it and opens modal with `trigger: "stripe_checkout_blocked"`; (c) **Persistent sticky banner** (`AnonUpgradeBanner`) across app pages — auto-hidden on `/login`, `/signup`, `/auth/*`; dismiss-X stores timestamp in localStorage `oi_anon_banner_dismissed_at` and re-shows after 7 days. PostHog event suite for funnel: `anon_upgrade_modal_shown`, `anon_upgrade_method_chosen`, `anon_upgrade_success`, `anon_upgrade_error`, `anon_upgrade_conflict`, `anon_upgrade_banner_clicked / _dismissed`. Required user-side Supabase toggle: "Allow manual linking" ON (was OFF by default — without it, `linkIdentity` silently fails for OAuth upgrades).

> **✅ ANON-AUTH-PHASE-2 Done 2026-06-10** — Lazy anonymous-user creation on first save action. `src/lib/anon-auth.ts` adds `ensureAnonUser(supabase, source)` helper — idempotent, returns existing session user if any, otherwise calls `signInAnonymously()` and returns the new anon user. `source` tag (`"favorite_match" | "tracker_pick" | …`) flows to PostHog so we can attribute anon signups to which engagement surface triggered them. Two write surfaces opened to logged-out visitors: (1) **`MatchFavoriteButton`** — was hidden for `!user` returning null; now visible to everyone, first click triggers ensureAnonUser → favorite write lands against the new user.id, no UI interruption (no modal, no redirect). (2) **`MatchPickButton`** — was rendering a "Sign up to make picks" CTA card; now renders the pick UI to anon users, first pick triggers ensureAnonUser. Both use the returned user object directly (not `useAuth()` post-create) so the favorite/pick lands instantly rather than waiting for the `onAuthStateChange` listener to propagate. PostHog tracking events: `anon_user_create_attempt`, `anon_user_created`, `anon_user_create_error`, `favorite_match_added / _removed`, `tracker_pick_made { selection }`.

> **✅ ANON-AUTH-PHASE-1 Done 2026-06-10** — Foundation for anonymous Supabase users. Migration 234: `ALTER TABLE profiles ALTER COLUMN email DROP NOT NULL` + `handle_new_user` trigger updated to pass `new.email` through unchanged (was `COALESCE(email, '')` which made anon rows look invalid) + defensive normalisation of any pre-existing `email=''` rows to NULL. Migration 235 — RLS hardening: enabled RLS on 9 tables flagged CRITICAL by Supabase Security Advisor (`tennis_value_bets`, `tennis_fixtures_today`, `manual_placement_queue`, `bet_telegram_alerts`, `odds_snapshots_quarantined`, `matches_dupe_quarantined`, `simulated_bets_pre_inplay_normalize_2026_05_17`, `cs2_hltv_team_stats`, `cs2_hltv_top_players`) — public sports data tables get `public_read`, internal/ops/quarantine tables get RLS-on-no-policies so only `service_role` can reach them. Same migration also adds JWT `is_anonymous` check to `match_votes` and `match_notes` write policies (anonymous users inherit the `authenticated` role per Supabase docs — without this, signInAnonymously is a vote-inflation / spam vector). Migration 236 — `cs2_pit_team_map` view recreated with `SECURITY INVOKER` (was `SECURITY DEFINER` which bypasses caller RLS). Frontend: `UserProfile.email` typed `string | null`, `useAuth()` exposes `isAnonymous` (truth source: `user.is_anonymous` from Supabase JWT, fallback to `profile.email == null`), PostHog `PostHogUserSync` skips `identify()` for anonymous users (avoids per-user billing) — uses `register({distinct_id, is_anonymous: true})` instead. Stripe checkout API returns 403 `anonymous_upgrade_required` for anon users — Stripe customer requires an email, anon users have none. Backend E2E test script `scripts/test_anon_auth_e2e.py` verified 12/12 checks pass (anon signup → profile row + email NULL + tier=free + can favorite + blocked from votes/notes + updateUser preserves user.id + favorite carries over to upgraded user). Test script then broken by Phase 4 captcha enforcement, kept for documentation. User-side Supabase toggle: "Allow anonymous sign-ins" ON.

> **✅ CS2-MATCH-DETAILS-THROUGHPUT Done 2026-06-10** — Match-details processor was crawling at ~7-10 matches/min, leaving the 8,200-row queue ~17h to drain. End-to-end probe showed `_fetch` averaged 5-7s (FlareSolverr full-page render every match) and `write_match` 6-8s (~30 individual psycopg2 round-trips to Supabase per match for the matches/maps/veto/player_stats child tables). **Two-stage refactor**: (1) **Fetch** — `flaresolverr_client.fetch_full()` now returns cookies + UA alongside the HTML; `_fetch()` warms via FlareSolverr once, captures `cf_clearance` + `__cf_bm`, then issues subsequent matches via plain `requests.Session` with cached cookies (~0.2-1s vs 5-7s). Re-warm trigger on 403/429/503 OR after `WARMUP_REUSE_LIMIT=60` calls / `WARMUP_MAX_AGE_S=900s` (well inside CF cookie TTL). (2) **Write** — `write_match()` now opens a single connection, runs the parent upsert + 3 child-table DELETEs in one statement, then `psycopg2.extras.execute_values()` for maps/veto/player_stats — 30 round-trips → 5, all in one transaction. Measured end-to-end after both: probe avg **0.55s/match** (was ~8s, **~15× faster**); live processor steady-state **~27-30 matches/min** vs 7.8 before (**~4× faster** end-to-end — bounded now by network jitter to Supabase + occasional re-warm). Queue ETA: **~5h instead of ~17h**. RATE_DELAY 0.5 → 0.2. Smoke `CS2-HLTV-MATCH-DETAILS` updated to pin `WARMUP_REUSE_LIMIT`, `_warmup_via_flaresolverr`, `_warm_session`, the `(403, 429, 503)` re-warm guard, and the `execute_values` + `with get_conn() as conn` batched-write pattern.

> **✅ COOLBET-PLACER-FLARESOLVERR-WIRE Done 2026-06-10** — Bridges the auth gap between the new FlareSolverr-based `scripts/coolbet/session_heartbeat.py` (shipped today as `13ca30f`) and the older `workers/automation/coolbet_placer.py` HTTP path. Symptom: `place_one_real_bet.py --execute` got past the menu + JWT-refresh steps but hit Imperva HTTP 500 on `search/v2` because the placer's `requests.Session()` was using stale `.env` Imperva cookies (`COOLBET_COOKIE_*` env vars) — the FlareSolverr login captured fresh cookies in its session memory but never wrote them back to `.env`. Manual-paste of `COOLBET_IMPERVA_COOKIES` from browser DevTools was the workaround, which defeats the whole point of the headless-Chrome auth pipeline. Fix: new `_persist_imperva_cookies(env_path, cookies)` helper in `flaresolverr_login_enroll.py` syncs the six `COOLBET_COOKIE_*` vars (REESE84, VISID_INCAP, NLBI, NLBI2, INCAP_SES, UUID) from FlareSolverr's harvested cookies to `.env` on every successful login (`cmd_start` no-SMS path AND `cmd_verify` SMS path, since both flow through `_persist_jwt`). Missing-cookie semantics: helper logs which env vars got fresh values vs were skipped; missing UUID is preserved (it's Coolbet's stable device-trust marker, doesn't rotate) rather than blanked. Critical-missing case (REESE84 or VISID_INCAP) prints a `[warn]` for operator visibility. Smoke: `COOLBET-PLACER-FLARESOLVERR-WIRE` pins helper existence, the `_persist_jwt → _persist_imperva_cookies` call chain (so both login paths benefit without duplication), all six env vars must appear in the helper, and each must also appear in `workers/automation/coolbet_session.py` (catches name-drift between writer and reader). Verified live: 4-of-6 cookies synced cleanly (REESE84 + VISID_INCAP + NLBI2 + INCAP_SES) — NLBI and UUID weren't in the warmup harvest. NLBI2 is the modern Imperva variant; UUID likely already in `.env` from earlier setup. **Real-money test deferred to operator** (per CLAUDE.md `--execute` safety memory) — implementation ready; user drives the actual `place_one_real_bet.py` invocation when they're ready.

> **✅ WC-PICKS-ONCONFLICT-FIX Done 2026-06-10** — User reported the WC group-standings predictor was failing to save with `there is no unique or exclusion constraint matching the ON CONFLICT specification`. Root cause: migration 170 replaced the human-side `UNIQUE` constraints on `wc_group_predictions`, `wc_bracket_picks`, and `wc_bracket_meta` with **partial** unique indexes (`WHERE user_id IS NOT NULL`) so AI-ghost rows could share the table. PostgREST's `.upsert(..., { onConflict: "..." })` can't infer a partial index — the JS client doesn't pass a WHERE predicate, so Postgres refuses with the error above. Same bug latent on all three tables; the bracket-save and bracket-meta upserts would have hit it next as kickoff approaches tomorrow. Fix: migration 228 drops the three partial human-side indexes and adds real `UNIQUE` constraints on `(user_id, group_letter, position)`, `(user_id, round, position)`, `(user_id)`. Default NULLS DISTINCT keeps the AI-ghost rows (user_id NULL) coexisting freely; their uniqueness is still enforced by the `WHERE ai_label IS NOT NULL` partial indexes left intact from migration 170. Smoke `WC-PICKS-ONCONFLICT-FIX` pins the three drops + three new constraints + cross-checks the FE `onConflict` strings haven't drifted from the constraint shapes.

> **✅ COOLBET-TEAM-ALIAS Done 2026-06-09** — User flagged Coolbet match 5603786 (`Northern Virginia FC vs Charlottesville Blues`) wasn't matching our `Evergreen FC vs Charlottesville Blues` row even though it's the same club — the USL League Two side rebranded from Evergreen FC to Northern Virginia FC / NoVa FC but plays out of the Evergreen Sportsplex and both names still circulate. Root cause: even when the away-prefix search returned the right candidate, `fuzzy_match_event` scored `min(home_score, away_score) = min(~27, 100) ≈ 27` (`partial_ratio("evergreen fc", "northern virginia fc")` shares no substring) — well below threshold 70. Fix: `_TEAM_ALIASES` map in `workers/automation/coolbet_placer.py` keyed on ASCII-folded lowercased names; `_team_aliases()` returns the original plus alternates; `search_coolbet_event` now expands prefix queries across every alias; `fuzzy_match_event` scores each side against every alias and takes the max. Bidirectional: Evergreen FC → Northern Virginia FC AND Northern Virginia FC → Evergreen FC (so a future DB rename doesn't silently re-break placement). Smoke: COOLBET-TEAM-ALIAS pins map registration, helper round-trip, and both directions of end-to-end fuzzy match. Existing COOLBET-SEARCH-LAVAL / COOLBET-FUZZY-DATE-GUARD / COOLBET-UNICODE-FUZZY all still pass.

> **✅ CS2-DATA-DEEP-EXPANSION Done 2026-06-09** — Massive scrape + modelling session. **15 commits**, **6 new migrations** (213-218), **5 new sneak-peek backtests**. **Data infrastructure**: fixed match-detail parser bugs (3 separate issues: team1==team2, maps empty, players 0), captures per-side CT/T halftime splits + per-(player, map) K-D/ADR/KAST/Rating (mig 213-214); HLTV `/results` queue walked 100 pages → ~10k matches queued; roster scraper `cs2_hltv_rosters.py` (mig 217) reads team page no-auth, captures days_in_team; clutch/comeback fields populated; point-in-time view `cs2_pit_team_map` (mig 218); Pinnacle scanner `cs2_pinnacle_scanner.py` via guest API (polite 4-6s jitter, Railway-only IP). **Self-healing**: `cs2_scraper_state` table (mig 215) + `scraper_state.py` context manager wrap all 9 scrapers (match_details_*, team_map_stats, player_stats, map_meta, hltv_rankings, player_ratings, clv_snapshot, team_rosters, pinnacle_scanner). ScrapersPanel on `/admin/cs2` shows progress/status/errors. 6h stuck-row reset. **Models (5 sneak peeks)**: `cs2_model_backtest_history` (mig 216) + BacktestPanel on `/admin/cs2`. **Best result: v5 (v4-ALL + bo_format) — AUC 0.688, +1.5pp over hltv_v1 production baseline (0.673)**. Stacking design: saved hltv_v1 prob as base + extras on top via logistic. Features that lift: rest_diff, h2h_diff, form_diff, tm_diff, bo_format. Features that don't: tournament_tier (too noisy), rank_diff (hltv_v1 already uses it). XGBoost LOSES on every feature set (Δ AUC -0.028 to -0.039) — overfits at 3.1k matches; logistic is right tool until ≥5-10k. **Railway scheduler**: match_details_process every 30min, queue 3x/day, sneak_peek_backtest daily 04:30 UTC, rosters daily 02:00 UTC, pinnacle every 30min 10-23 UTC. **Pending**: team_name on player_match_stats (blocks avg_team_rating_pit feature); Pinnacle leagues need Railway readback; match-details backfill at 148/9,713 (~1.5d to drain); smoke tests for new components (tech debt).

Fix: added a heuristic in `DisagreementCallout` (`wc-model-card.tsx`) that detects strings starting with `{` (bare JSON) or `<word> {` (prefixed JSON like `wc_a4_blend {...}`) and falls back to the generic ELO/Poisson explainer. > **✅ WC-MODEL-CARD-COPY-DIRECTION Done 2026-06-06** — Match-detail WC model card was telling users "Our model agrees on X but is **more confident**" even when our model was clearly LESS confident than the market (own_prob home = 49.87% / market home = 75.46% on `a7dc0b87-c36f-42d8-8fcc-10c34b2f5f59`). Root cause: `DisagreementCallout` in `src/components/wc-model-card.tsx` triggered on `Math.abs(gapPp) >= 10` (fires in either direction) but the copy branch was hard-coded to "more confident" whenever the picks agreed. Fix: derive `ownIsMoreConfident = info.gapPp >= 0` from the signed gap and pick between "more confident" / "less confident" wording. The "model leans X, market favours Y" branch (different picks) is unchanged. Smoke `WC-MODEL-CARD-COPY-DIRECTION` pins both directional strings + the signed-gap gate so a future copy refactor cannot regress. Note: the user-reported "58%" matches the blended row (λ=0.33 → 0.67·0.4987 + 0.33·0.7546 ≈ 0.58); the callout itself uses the own row (50%) — both readings make the "more confident" wording wrong.

> **✅ BOT-AGGRESSIVE-V2-DECIDE Done 2026-06-06** — Retired via `UPDATE bots SET is_active=false, retired_at=NOW(), retired_reason='…'`. Final tally: 59 settled bets, 39.0% hit rate, **−21.95% ROI / −€93.49 net P&L**, **CLV +6.46%**. Positive CLV with sharply negative ROI signals a win-rate-at-odds problem — model finds edge vs closing line but bets are skewed toward odds where the edge can't overcome the implied break-even rate (e.g., needs 50%+ hit rate on 2.00 odds, gets 39%). Daily betting pipeline already skips `is_active=false` bots so no further code change needed. dashboard_cache will pick up the change on next 30-min refresh. > **⬜ PUBLIC-MATURITY-BADGE — defer until value-bets density audit complete** — User-facing version of the calibration/bot maturity signal for `/value-bets` and match detail page. Operator and user contexts require **different framing**: telling users "BET" implies advice we can't legally make (gambling-advice regulation varies by jurisdiction) and ethically shouldn't (we have calibrated edges, not winners — even +8% CLV markets lose 45-50% of the time). Use **descriptive labels only**: e.g. a small 10-12px colored dot beside the market chip (🟢 mature / 🟡 calibrating / ⚪ experimental) with hover/click revealing the model + bot facts as numbers (ECE / sample / CLV / ROI). **Dependency:** user observed value-bets rows are already visually dense (PRO chips, edge%, time, units, community dots, market chip all on one row); adding a verbose chip would compound noise. Best to ship this alongside a value-bets density refactor — file as `VALUE-BETS-DENSITY-PASS`. Effort: ~3-4h once design pass is done.
>
> **✅ VALUE-BETS-DENSITY-PASS Done 2026-06-07** — Collapsed row decluttered: removed "Pre-match" chip (default state, not informative), removed ConsensusDots from collapsed row, kept "In-play" chip only when `bet.isInplay`. Moved "Calibrated" badge and league hit rate badge into `ExpandedPanel` so the pick line stays clean on scroll. `ExpandedPanel` received a new `leagueHitRate` prop passed down from `ValueBetRow`. Smoke `VALUE-BETS-DENSITY-PASS` pins: no Pre-match in collapsed row, In-play present, Calibrated + hit rate in ExpandedPanel.
>
> **✅ INPLAY-I-INVESTIGATE Done 2026-06-06** — Investigated `inplay_i` (Favourite Stall) at −36.5% ROI / 17.6% hit rate over 17 bets. Two root causes identified, both fixed same-day:
>
> **(1) Missing Bayesian update on prematch xG.** Strategy I computed `model_fav_win` via bivariate Poisson on `lam = pm_xg × remaining_frac × h2_uplift` — i.e. it treated the lambdas as if the match hadn't started, IGNORING the information in the observed 0-0 score at minute 42-65. A 1.8-xG team scoring 0 by minute 42 has demonstrated under-performance; the posterior λ must shrink. Strategy J (`_check_strategy_j`) already does this with a conjugate Gamma update (s=2 prior). Strategy I did NOT. Fix: applied identical Gamma update (`posterior_xg = (pm_xg × s + goals_observed) / (s + minute/90)`) before the bivariate Poisson call. Sim confirmed the update reduces `model_fav_win` by ~4-5pp across the bot's actual operating range (pm_xg 1.5-1.9, minute 42-65) — enough to kill marginal-edge bets while leaving genuinely strong picks intact.
>
> **(2) Low-data-league overconfidence (same family as inplay_n).** All 17 bets fired in low-tier / friendly leagues (Victoria Premier 2, Erovnuli Liga 2, Friendlies, Paulista Série B, Regionalliga - Mitte, USL League One, Torneo Federal A, etc.) — NONE in top-5 European leagues. In these leagues the prematch ensemble's `predictions.win_prob` ≥ 0.62 is systematically over-confident relative to where the market priced the same side. Fix: copied inplay_n's `pm_fav_prob − pm_implied_fav > 0.15` gate into `_check_strategy_i`. Uses the `prematch_implied_home/away` columns already exposed in `_get_prematch_data` by today's earlier inplay_n work.
>
> **Note on the 17 bets:** Even with both fixes, the bot's historical ROI cannot be undone — these are sunk paper losses. The fixes prevent the SAME failure mode going forward. Expect fewer bets to fire (the marginal-edge cohort gets skipped) and better hit rate among those that do. Monitor over next 20-30 bets; if ROI doesn't recover to break-even, retire and revisit. Smoke `INPLAY-I-BAYES-XG` pins both fixes — Bayesian update lines locality-scoped to `_check_strategy_i` so a future refactor can't silently drop them. `INPLAY-I-FAV-STALL` (pre-existing) still green — no regression on the core contract.
>
> **✅ INPLAY-CALIBRATED-PROB-WIRE Done 2026-06-06** — Spotted while diagnosing inplay_i: all 898 historical in-play bets have `simulated_bets.calibrated_prob = NULL`, including the 398 inplay_e bets that today's `inplay_e_under_25` Platt row was supposed to calibrate. Root cause: `_build_inplay_bet_data` never copied `cal_model_prob` from `trigger.extra` to the top-level `bet_data["calibrated_prob"]`. Strategy E was computing it via `apply_platt(raw, "inplay_e_under_25", odds)` and storing it inside the JSON `reasoning` blob, but the dedicated DB column stayed NULL. Fix: 4 lines in `_build_inplay_bet_data` propagate the value when present, leave the key absent when missing (so strategies that don't call apply_platt write honest NULL, not a falsely-equal-to-model_probability sentinel). **Secondary fix shipped same change (FIT-PLATT-INPLAY-EXCLUDE):** added `match_minute_at_pick IS NULL` filter to `scripts/fit_platt.py` OU + BTTS queries AND `scripts/threshold_check.py` CAL-PLATT rows. Without this filter, populated in-play `calibrated_prob` would have leaked into next Sunday's prematch Platt fit and corrupted it (in-play uses Bayesian posterior on live xG; prematch uses pre-kickoff features; same `simulated_bets.market='o/u'` tag for both). Smoke `INPLAY-CALIBRATED-PROB-WIRE` pins the propagation rule; `FIT-PLATT-THRESHOLD-CONTRACT` extended to pin the new `match_minute_at_pick IS NULL` filter on both files. Threshold_check post-fix output confirms sane numbers (AH dropped 332→305 indicating ~27 in-play AH bets correctly excluded from the prematch-only count).
>
> **⬜ INPLAY-CALIBRATION-COMPLETE — target rolling, as samples land** — Broader follow-up to INPLAY-CALIBRATED-PROB-WIRE. Today only Strategy E calls `apply_platt`; the other 12 in-play strategies (a/b/c/d/g/h/i/j/l/m/n/o/p/p_v2) write raw `model_probability` with no calibration. For each strategy, once it crosses the ≥100-settled-bet gate, we need to: (1) define a stable per-strategy market_key (e.g. `inplay_i_1x2_home`, `inplay_p_1x2_home`); (2) wire `apply_platt(raw, market_key, odds)` into the strategy's bet builder; (3) fit Platt via a script analogous to `scripts/fit_platt_inplay_e.py`. Current sample sizes per bot (settled bets, 2026-06-06): `inplay_p=193` ✅ already past gate, `inplay_o=83`, `inplay_c=53`, `inplay_l=33`, `inplay_n=25`, `inplay_i=17`, others <15. **Order of operations**: inplay_p first (largest sample, also persistent negative ROI — investigate + calibrate in one go). Effort ~3-4h per strategy; ~12-16h total across all. Do them as data lands, not in one batch.
>
> **⬜ INPLAY-I-POST-FIX-WATCH — target after 20-30 new bets (~3-4 weeks)** — Verify INPLAY-I-INVESTIGATE fixes (Bayesian update + market gate) actually improve hit-rate. Query: `SELECT COUNT(*), SUM(CASE WHEN result='won' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), SUM(pnl) FROM simulated_bets sb JOIN bots b ON b.id=sb.bot_id WHERE b.name='inplay_i' AND sb.created_at >= '2026-06-06 18:00 UTC' AND sb.result IN ('won','lost')`. Hit-rate target: ≥25% (break-even at avg 4.0 odds). If after 30 settled bets it's still <20%, retire — strategy thesis was wrong, not just the math. If 25-30%, keep watching. If ≥30%, consider promoting to higher confidence cohort.
>

Investigation verified the retirement decision was correct AND surfaced one defect in v2 that's NOT being fixed today (gated on more data).

> | Bucket | n | W | PnL | ROI |

> | 2.50-2.99 | 28 | 6 | −€58.24 | **−41.6%** |

> **(2) v2 working but on thin sample.** 47 settled bets / +€68.79 / **+29.3% ROI**. Profit concentrated in 4.00-4.99 bucket (+81.2% / 20 bets); 3.00-3.99 bucket flipped slightly negative vs v1 (−5.0% / 21 bets vs +3.9% / 48 bets) — could be noise.
>
> **(3) Same Bayesian-update defect as inplay_i/j present in v2.** `_check_strategy_p_v2` uses `lambda_eq * remaining_frac` directly — no Gamma posterior update conditioning on the 1-1 score state. **NOT FIXED TODAY** — sim showed the fix WOULD shift edge by 1-3pp (smaller than inplay_i's 4-5pp because observed goals at 1-1 are close to expected), but with only 47 bets we can't verify the change doesn't kill the working +81% bucket. Risk of breaking what works > value of theoretical correctness at this sample size. **Filed as gated follow-up — see INPLAY-P-V2-100-BET-CHECK below.**

> ## 2026-06-06 — INPLAY-O-QUARANTINE
>
> **✅ INPLAY-O-QUARANTINE Done 2026-06-06** — User flagged `inplay_o` as suspiciously over-performing (€591.95 PnL vs €9.79 for the next-best bot, 60× the cohort median). Investigation: bot's "Underdog Hold" strategy fired €17–€26 odds on "Andorra (home) leading 1-0 vs Iraq" / "Jamaica (home) leading 2-0 vs India" etc. — at minutes 25-55 those odds for a leading team are physically impossible (leader should compress, not drift). Root cause: API-Football's `goals.home/away` field lags the bookmaker's reaction to a goal by 30-60s; when both score and odds get fetched in the same tick, the score column shows the pre-goal state while the live 1x2 odds already reflect the post-goal market. Bot reads "home leading 1-0" (stale) + "home odds 17.00" (fresh, drifted-long because the OTHER team scored), trips the strategy, places a bet at 17.00, and settlement (which sees the correct final score) marks it WON if the team it picked actually won the match by coincidence. The score-odds veto `_score_odds_consistent()` was added 2026-05-30 (commit 6cf9a6a) and stops new occurrences; this task back-tested + quarantined the pre-fix damage. Back-test (1x2 only): of 70 pre-fix inplay_o bets with snapshot data, **62 were placed on inverted snapshots** (88.6%); 22 of those "won" generating **€577.51 of fake PnL out of €822.57 total wins (70%)**. Quarantined all 62 inverted bets (result→'void', pnl→0), recomputed `bots.current_bankroll` from €1591.95 → €1214.44, force-refreshed `dashboard_cache` so frontend reflects the corrected numbers immediately. All-bots scan confirmed contamination was concentrated entirely in inplay_o (only 2 inverted bets across all other 1x2 bots in 60d, both lost). The inplay_o strategy specifically targets the data state where the AF score-lag is most damaging (high-odds underdog "leading"). Backup of voided rows: `/tmp/inplay_o_quarantine_backup.csv`. Smoke: INPLAY-O-QUARANTINE (regression guard — asserts no post-fix inplay_o bets snuck through + the 62 void cohort remains in place).
>
> **⬜ INPLAY-O-FOLLOWUP-AUDIT** — After 30 more days of post-fix inplay_o data (target 2026-07-06), re-run the bot ROI math. If `inplay_o` still posts > 20% ROI on clean data, the strategy may have genuine edge worth scaling. If it regresses to flat/negative, retire alongside `inplay_p`. Decision deferred to ensure a meaningful sample post-veto.
>

> ## 2026-06-06 — COOLBET-SEARCH-SPORT-FILTER
>
> **✅ COOLBET-SEARCH-SPORT-FILTER Done 2026-06-06** — User flagged Shanghai Port II vs Shanghai Second (China League Two, 2026-06-06 11:00 EET, bot_high_alignment double_chance/x2) showing `⚠ no market` in /admin/place even though Coolbet's site clearly listed the fixture (`Shanghai Port B - Shanghai Segenda`, event 5609979) with the Topeltvõimalus market. Root cause: Coolbet's `/s/sbgate/sports/search/v2` returns events across every sport (each carries `sport_id` / `sport_category_id`), and `_do_search` was passing the raw payload straight to `_parse_event`. The basketball game `Shanghai - Liaoning` (id 5620895, `sport_category_id=65035`) leaked into the fuzzy-match candidate pool, and `fuzz.partial_ratio` scored both teams 100 (`"shanghai"` is a full substring of `"shanghai port ii"` AND of `"shanghai second"` — basketball "Shanghai vs Liaoning" → min(100, 100) = 100). The football match scored only ~86 so basketball won. The placer then fetched the basketball event's `Match Point Handicap` / `Match Total Points` markets, dropped every market as unknown in `parse_market`, and wrote only the 1X2 presence-marker snapshot — leaving `coolbetOdds=null` for DC/x2 and the frontend correctly degrading to `no_market`. DB confirms: 15 Coolbet snapshots for this match, all `1x2` (no DC, no AH, no OU, no BTTS), while the actual football event offers 26 markets including DC. Fix: filter `raw_events` in `_do_search` to only `sport_category_id == _FOOTBALL_CATEGORY_ID` (62) before parsing, so non-football candidates never reach fuzzy_match_event. Verified with probe: now matches `Shanghai Port B vs Shanghai Segenda` at score 86, resolves DC/x2 → odds 2.17. Smoke: COOLBET-SEARCH-SPORT-FILTER.
>
> **⬜ COOLBET-SEARCH-SPORT-FILTER-FOLLOWUP** — Investigate why early-cohort pick lists skew toward AH / DC bets across many fixtures. User observed many early KO games have only AH or DC paper bets in the queue. Could be (a) cohort timing — fresh-odds refresh hits 1X2/OU later, (b) bot universe — fewer 1X2/OU bots fire at the early cohort, or (c) data-side gaps (missing Pinnacle on these books at the time of run). Worth a single-day audit of `simulated_bets` market mix vs hour-of-day. Logged as a follow-up — separate from the no-market fix above.

>
> **✅ COOLBET-INGEST-ANON Done 2026-06-04** — User flagged France vs Ivory Coast (Coolbet event 5575227, live on their site) showing `⚠ no match` across every AH line in `/admin/place`. When `COOLBET_MANUAL_JWT` expired on 2026-05-28, the 30-min snapshot job (`:03/:33 UTC`) started raising `RuntimeError: COOLBET_MANUAL_JWT is expired` before any `search/v2` call — so no Coolbet `odds_snapshots` got written for any fixture added after that date. The frontend's `matchIdsWithCoolbetEvent` set was empty → every row fell through to `no_event` (`engine-data.ts:1929`). But Coolbet's `search/v2`, `fo-category`, `fo-match`, and `sidebets` endpoints all work with Imperva cookies only — `--record` mode (placer) has been using `require_auth=False` since COOLBET-ANON-READ (commit `673b9e1`). The ingest path just never got the same treatment. Switched all three explorer entry points + `scripts/coolbet_match_probe.py` to `CoolbetSession(require_auth=False)`. Verified with the live probe: `France vs Ivory Coast` matches at score 100, `resolve(asian_handicap, away -1)` → odds 3.42 placement target. Placer's actual bet POST keeps the authed default (only path that genuinely needs a live cbauth). Smoke: COOLBET-INGEST-ANON (pins all four sites + the `require_auth` short-circuit in `_ensure_auth`).
>
> ## 2026-06-04 — CSV-FULL-EXTRACT + DRIFT-FEATURE + AH-BOT-PROTOTYPE
>
> **✅ CSV-FULL-EXTRACT Done 2026-06-04** — Audit found 479 football-data.co.uk CSVs on disk with 120 columns/row but `extract_odds_rows()` only stored 4 (Pinnacle + Bet365 1X2 closing + OU 2.5 closing). Rewrote to capture 9 bookmakers (added Betfair Exchange, Max/Avg consensus, BetWin, Betfred, William Hill, 1xBet) across 1X2/OU2.5/AH markets, closing **and** opening lines, with `handicap_line` set on AH rows. Also backfilled match stats (HS/HST/HC/HY/HR/HF + away counterparts) into `match_stats` and `matches.referee` where AF was NULL. Perf rewrite to hit usable throughput: bulk in-memory match-lookup (~150x faster than per-row find_match), COPY for the big odds_snapshots insert (~3-5x faster than execute_values), single pinned connection per league-season, scoped existing-snapshot dedup query. Found + fixed Pinnacle prefix bug in extraction (1X2 uses PSC/PS but OU/AH uses PC/P) via smoke test before publishing the ingest. Final row deltas: ~118K Betfair Exchange (was 0), ~80K Max, ~80K Avg, ~18K Pinnacle AH closing with line, ~23K Bet365 AH closing with line, ~17K Betfair Exchange AH closing with line, plus opening rows across the same books. Backtest results in `dev/active/csv-full-extract-backtest-results.md`: (a) **Pinnacle vs Betfair Exchange as shrinkage anchor — identical to 4 decimals** (Brier 0.5886/0.5887 on 7,328 paired matches), no anchor swap warranted; (b) **AH market efficient** at flat stakes (home −5.4%, away +0.9% over 8,868 matches) — but the backtest universe now exists for AH bot development; (c) **Pinnacle open→close drift produces a +8.76pp home win-rate spread top vs bottom quintile** on 8,850 matches — strong monotonic signal worth materialising as a meta-model feature. Smoke: CSV-FULL-EXTRACT (extract_odds_rows + extract_match_stats unit test).
>
> **✅ DRIFT-FEATURE Done 2026-06-04** — Migration 179 adds `pinnacle_drift_home/draw/away` to `match_feature_vectors`. New `scripts/backfill_pinnacle_drift.py` joins is_opening + is_closing Pinnacle 1X2 rows and computes `close_implied − open_implied` per selection, bulk UPDATEs MFV in one round-trip via VALUES table. Distinct from existing `pinnacle_line_move_*_at_t6h` (migration 128) which uses a ≤T−6h cutoff that excludes CSV closing snapshots timestamped at kickoff. Open question for follow-up (DRIFT-FEATURE-APPLY): once backfill runs against the ~8,850-match CSV-historical population, add the new column to `FEATURE_COLS` in `workers/model/train.py` so the next Sunday weekly retrain picks it up. Result-doc cited the 8.76pp WR spread as the empirical justification.
>
> **✅ AH-BOT-PROTOTYPE Done 2026-06-04** — `scripts/backtest_ah_bot_prototype.py` ran on 5,254 matches with paired Pinnacle AH closing + ensemble 1X2 prediction (lines restricted to ±0.5, ±0.25, 0 — subset cleanly derivable from 1X2 alone). Result: **negative ROI at every edge threshold**, getting worse as the filter tightens (−2.65% at 0 edge → −5.01% at +10% edge). Classic noise-not-signal signature. Naive "ensemble 1X2 → AH probability" approach does not beat Pinnacle's AH market. Filed (`dev/active/ah-bot-prototype-results.md`) the negative finding plus a follow-up sketch — a real AH bot needs a dedicated goals model (Poisson + team form), lineup/injury/drift signal layers, or non-Pinnacle anchor. **AH-BOT-MODEL** (the proper goals-model variant) deferred to post-WC. Strategic value of this run: confirms Pinnacle anchor is sharp; rules out the cheap version of an AH bot before sinking model effort.
>
> **✅ DRIFT-FEATURE-APPLY Done 2026-06-04** — Migration 179 + fix-forward 183 applied. `scripts/backfill_pinnacle_drift.py` populated 260 of 10,260 paired open+close Pinnacle matches with drift values (the rest don't have MFV rows yet — MFV table starts 2026-04 via live pipeline). `train.py` gained `DRIFT_FEATURE_COLS` + `--include-drift` flag mirroring `--include-pinnacle`. `scheduler.py` weekly retrain initially passed `--include-drift` but REVERTED 2026-06-04 (see DRIFT-FEATURE-REENABLE below) to avoid confounding the June 7 / June 15 calibration + bot-threshold work with a feature that's 97% NULL at current coverage. The capability is wired; the auto-activation is paused.
>
> **⬜ DRIFT-FEATURE-REENABLE** — Re-enable `--include-drift` on the auto Sunday retrain in `workers/scheduler.py:job_weekly_retrain`. Trigger conditions: (a) the Batch 2 calibration eval (target 2026-06-15) is baselined cleanly without drift, AND (b) MFV coverage on `pinnacle_drift_home` is ≥30% of training rows (currently 2.6%). Coverage grows two ways: live pipeline writes opening + closing Pinnacle snapshots for every new match in our window, and we can build an MFV-historical backfill that fills MFV rows for the 10K CSV-paired matches. Earliest realistic re-enable date: 2026-06-22 retrain. Activation step is a 3-line edit + comment removal + commit.
>
> **✅ AH-BOT-MODEL Done 2026-06-05** — `scripts/backtest_ah_bot_model.py` ran the production Poisson + Dixon-Coles AH function (`_ah_model_prob` from daily_pipeline_v2.py) against the full 8,441-match Pinnacle AH backtest universe (all line types — whole / half / quarter). Inverts ensemble (p_h, p_d) via `_solve_lambdas_calibrated()` to recover (exp_h, exp_a), then computes fair AH probabilities at the actual handicap line. Result: **negative ROI at every edge threshold**, monotonically worsening (−2.93% at 0 edge → −4.65% at +0.10 edge). Same textbook noise-not-signal pattern as the naive prototype. Two consistent observations across both backtests: (a) ensemble systematically rates away sides higher than Pinnacle AH implied — N(away bets) ≈ 2.1× N(home bets) at zero edge, suggesting a residual away bias the Platt calibration leaves; (b) Pinnacle AH closing is too sharp for our current model. **Both the cheap path and the proper goals-model path confirm: no edge over Pinnacle AH closing.** Results in `dev/active/ah-bot-model-results.md`. **Verdict: shelve AH bot development.** Future revisit options filed (line movement / soft-book disagreement / dedicated AH goals model / lineup signals — each its own substantial project). The 8,868-match AH closing universe remains a reusable asset for any future AH model attempt.
>
> **⬜ CSV-HT-GOALS — backlog (gated on HT model decision)** — Extract half-time goals (`HTHG`/`HTAG`) from football-data.co.uk CSVs. Pre-requisite for half-time prediction markets (HT 1X2, HT/FT, first-half OU, HT BTTS), none of which are in BOTS_CONFIG today. Implementation: migration adds `ht_score_home`/`ht_score_away` to `matches`; extend `extract_match_stats()` to grab HTHG/HTAG; re-run idempotent ingest. ~30min once the HT model decision is committed. **Not actionable until HT infra is on the roadmap** — moved to backlog 2026-06-06 audit pass.

> ## 2026-06-03 — COOLBET-SELECTION-CASE
>
> **✅ COOLBET-SELECTION-CASE Done 2026-06-03** — `/admin/place` flagged every Coolbet 1X2 + DC bet as `⚠ no market` even when Coolbet had the event and the market. Root cause: `coolbet_explorer.parse_market` + `_normalise_our_target` emitted Title-case selections (`Home`/`Away`/`Draw`/`1X`/`X2`) that got written into `odds_snapshots`, but the frontend's `_mapPaperToSnapshotKey` lowercases (`home`/`away`/`1x`/`x2`) to match every OTHER bookmaker's storage convention. Lookup missed → `coolbetOdds = null` → match had a Coolbet snapshot row so badge fell through to `no_market`. Confirmed with Olympic vs Lund (Sweden / Ettan - Södra, KO 2026-06-03 17:00 UTC, bot_v10_all 1x2 home @ 2.52): Coolbet snapshot `1x2/Home @ 2.33` captured 07:14 UTC today, but frontend showed ⚠ no market. DB-wide: 2,800 Coolbet rows (1x2 + double_chance) had capital case; every other bookmaker was already lowercase. Fix: `parse_market` + `_normalise_our_target` + `_outcome_id_for_selection` + `_write_presence_marker_snapshot` now emit/consume lowercase across 1X2 + DC; one-off `UPDATE odds_snapshots SET selection = LOWER(selection)` backfilled the existing 2,800 rows. New smoke COOLBET-SELECTION-CASE pins parse_market + _normalise_our_target + end-to-end resolve. Updated smokes: COOLBET-MARKET-NORM, COOLBET-ODDS-SNAPSHOT, COOLBET-MARKET-TYPE-IDS, COOLBET-NO-MARKET-PRESENCE, DC-CASE-AND-RESULTKEY-FIX.

Reuses the existing `match_previews` table (migration 033) — distinct match_ids vs. the club preview job so no clash; the match-detail page's existing `getMatchPreview()` picks up WC previews automatically. New cron registered at `07:30 UTC` daily, gated inside `job_wc_match_previews` to `2026-06-04 ≤ today ≤ 2026-07-19` so APScheduler doesn't burn Gemini quota the rest of the year. Idempotent — fixtures whose preview is < 24h old are skipped; `--force` regenerates everything in the window. Rate-limited to 1 req/sec; Gemini errors caught per-fixture (loop never crashes). Frontend: new `getWorldCupPreviews()` batch-loader in `src/lib/world-cup.ts`; `/world-cup` schedule + group cards render a native `<details>` "AI preview" expander under each fixture row (collapsed by default, free to all tiers — lead-gen SEO content). Cost: ~728 calls × ~$0.01 ≈ **$7 per tournament cycle**. Deferred: post-match recap (1-2 sentences); multi-language. Smoke: WC-AI-PREVIEW-JOB + WC-AI-PREVIEW-CRON + WC-AI-PREVIEW-FE.

> ## 2026-06-02 — WORLD-CUP-PREP spike done; phased plan filed; Pro tier rethink deferred
>
> 9 days from kickoff. Spike (2026-06-02) confirmed: AF has 72 WC group-stage fixtures available via `get_fixtures_by_league_season(1, 2026)` but **zero are currently in our DB** (pipeline only pulls today's date). National-team data thin (73 friendlies in DB; AF has Euro 2024 + WC 2022 + WC quals ~320 matches available to backfill). Existing club-trained model won't handle national teams — needs separate ELO + Poisson predictor. No bookmaker odds (AF says no, Odds API not configured for WC). Pro tier rethink ran in parallel — Top-N hypothesis killed (-10% ROI over 3 years), "95% bankers" killed (model calibration ceiling at 66% WR), bot-maturity + prob-threshold intersection is the best lead (+10.36% ROI n=314 backtest, but needs re-validation against live simulated_bets). **All findings written to `dev/active/world-cup-prep-{plan,context,tasks}.md` + `dev/active/pro-tier-rethink-findings.md`** so we don't lose state across context compressions.
>
> **✅ WC-PHASE-1 Done 2026-06-02** — Backfilled 72 WC 2026 group-stage fixtures. Added `--league`/`--season` CLI to `workers/jobs/fetch_fixtures.py` (backfill mode skips daily-featured + ops_snapshot, passes today's date as log run_date to satisfy DATE column). Migration 163 flips `show_on_frontend=true` on the WC league row (was hidden despite `is_active=true` + `coverage_predictions=true`). Matches land with `season=2025` (our football-season convention: June = previous year) — this is correct and the frontend filters by date+`show_on_frontend`, not season. Smoke: WC-FIXTURES-IN-DB.
>
> **✅ WC-PHASE-2 Done 2026-06-02** — `scripts/backfill_internationals.py` ran across 59 (league, season) tuples, **6,921 fixtures stored (6,651 finished)** covering WC 2018/2022, Euro 2020/2024 + qual, Copa America, AFCON, Asian Cup, Gold Cup, all 4 UEFA Nations League editions, all 6 confederations' WC qualifiers for 2022 + 2026 cycles, Friendlies 2022-25, regional comps. Phase A (fixtures) complete; Phase B (lineups/events/stats/players) runs at ~4 matches/min because of per-match SQL fan-out — left running in background (~25h ETA), but Phase 3 (ELO + Poisson predictor) only needs scores + dates + teams which are all already in. Follow-up `OPT-BACKFILL-INTL` filed in `dev/active/world-cup-prep-tasks.md` to parallelise the enrichment post-WC. Smoke: INTL-BACKFILL.
>
> **✅ WC-PHASE-3 Done 2026-06-02** — National-team predictor (separate parallel model — club model's league/season features don't map to internationals). Stack: ELO walked over 6,651 finished international matches (K=40 for tournament / K=25 for qualifier+NL / K=10 for friendly, home_advantage +60 only on qualifier_nl); Poisson goals model from last-20-internationals weighted by competition. **1X2 backtest on 141 holdout matches (WC 2022 + Euro 2024 + Copa 2024 + AFCON 2023): log-loss 1.0697 vs 1.0986 baseline (+2.6% improvement), 45.4% top-pick accuracy (vs 33.3%), Euro 2024 hit rate 63.6%, WC 2022 48.4%.** Best config from sweep: softening_factor=1.3, draw_base=0.30. Goals model is calibrated but doesn't beat 50/50 baseline meaningfully — **ship 1X2 only; goals shown as informational, not as confidence claims**. Files: `workers/model/national_team_predictor.py`, `scripts/compute_international_elo.py`, `scripts/backtest_national_team_model.py`, migration 164 (`team_elo_international`). ELO ranking sanity-checks well: Spain #1, Morocco #2, France #3, England #4, Argentina #5 at the top; Andorra/Gibraltar/San Marino/Liechtenstein at the bottom. Smoke: NATIONAL-TEAM-PREDICTOR-V1.

> **✅ SEASON-CONVENTION-FIX Done 2026-06-02** — `bulk_store_matches` + `store_match` used to compute `season` from match_date alone (Jan-Jun → year-1, Jul-Dec → year). Correct for club leagues (PL 2024-25 = season=2024) but inverts the year for summer tournaments — WC 2026 fixtures (June 2026) landed as season=2025, WC 2018 split between 2017 + 2018, Euro 2024 partially under 2023. Fix: prefer AF's `league.season` value when present (already passed through `fixture_to_match_dict`), fall back to convention only when missing. Migration 167 renormalises existing rows for WC (1), Euro (4), AFCON (6), Asian Cup (7), Copa America (9), Gold Cup (22) — sets `season = EXTRACT(YEAR FROM date)`. Qualifiers NOT touched (multi-year, convention works). Smoke: SEASON-CONVENTION-FIX.

> ## 2026-06-01 — Cherry-pick Phase 2 open questions (decide 2026-06-05/06)
>
> User confirmed direction: real_bets should follow most-profitable bots / profiles, decision target = post-Phase-3.5 (Sunday 2026-06-07). Two unresolved design choices need a call BEFORE the 06-07 env flip, otherwise the gate behaves in ways we didn't intend.
>
> **Q1 — bot-level vs profile-level gate.** Phase 1 filter is `bots.maturity_label = ANY(...)`, per-bot. But several active bots have multiple `strategy_profile` values (bot_ou_specialist has Under 2.5 Specialist + Over 2.5 Sweden + Over 3.5 Global; bot_dnb_specialist had DNB Home + DNB Away). Per-bot promotion includes profiles that haven't earned it (e.g. Over 3.5 Global is unproven). Per-profile promotion would need a new column or a `(bot_id, strategy_profile)` allowlist. Recommend: per-bot in Phase 1, add per-profile in Phase 2 only if a multi-profile bot has clear winning + losing profile splits in shadow_bets. Filed as ⬜ open.
>
> **Q2 — starting allowlist is currently too narrow.** Today only 3 *actively-firing* bots are tagged `calibrated`: bot_v10_all (strong), bot_1x2_specialist (n=4 last 30d, marginal), inplay_e (n=215 / +8% ROI). Flipping `COOLBET_RECORD_ALLOWED_MATURITY=calibrated` on 06-08 would shrink real_bets to ~5-10 bets/day. Two paths to widen sensibly:
>   • Promote 2-3 more bots to `calibrated` via `/admin/promotion-candidates` (Phase 2 ships 06-05/06). Likely candidates per today's data: bot_aggressive_v2 (+57% last 7d but n=8 — needs more samples), bot_high_roi_global_v2 (+35% last 30d on n=12), bot_opt_home_lower (+52% n=20 last 30d).
>   • Start with `active,calibrated` as the env value; tighten to `calibrated` only after a week of clean data.
> Recommend path 1 (manual promotion) so the maturity_label semantics stay tight — but the decision waits until promotion criteria are written in Phase 2.
>
> Both questions land in `dev/active/cherry-pick-placer-plan.md` for the Phase 2 work.

> ## 2026-06-01 — EMAIL-DELIVERY-CHECK ran live; DMARC gap surfaced
>
> Verified Resend deliverability for `oddsintel.app` via the rewritten `scripts/check_email_deliverability.py`. Script now uses Resend's `/domains` API as authoritative record list (avoids guessing selector / subdomain) and cross-checks each expected record against live DNS. Live run:
>   • ✅ DKIM TXT at `resend._domainkey.oddsintel.app` (root) — matches Resend key
>   • ✅ SPF MX at `send.oddsintel.app` → `feedback-smtp.eu-west-1.amazonses.com`
>   • ✅ SPF TXT at `send.oddsintel.app` → `v=spf1 include:amazonses.com ~all`
>   • **❌ DMARC missing** at both root and `send` subdomain — recommended fix surfaced in script output
>
> **Action item for operator** (Cloudflare DNS — manual): add TXT record:
>   ```
>   _dmarc.oddsintel.app  TXT  "v=DMARC1; p=none; rua=mailto:postmaster@oddsintel.app; pct=100"
>   ```
> Start with `p=none` (monitoring-only — no impact on delivery). After 14d of clean DMARC aggregate reports, raise to `p=quarantine`. Major mailbox providers (Gmail, Outlook, Yahoo) increasingly require DMARC for senders >5K msgs/day. We're well below that, but with the Telegram alerts + value bet emails this trip threshold faster than expected. Original script had bugs (looked for DKIM as CNAME when Resend uses TXT, checked SPF on root domain when Resend uses `send` subdomain) — both fixed. Smoke: EMAIL-DELIVERY-CHECK.

> ## 2026-06-01 — SCHEDULER-HANG-MITIGATION post-mortem + partial fix
>
> Root cause of the 14:35 UTC scheduler hang identified. APScheduler is configured with `max_workers=4` in the default executor (workers/scheduler.py:1161). Three long-running jobs share the same 30-min firing minute under that constraint:
>   • `betting_refresh_interval` — fires `:05/:35` every hour 07-22 UTC
>   • `shadow_interval` — fires `:05/:35` every hour 07-22 UTC
>   • `coolbet_odds_interval` — fires `:03/:33` every hour 07-22 UTC (often still running into `:05/:35`)
>
> Plus `manual_placement_drain` (every 10s) and `hist_backfill` / `backfill_coaches` / `backfill_transfers` (every 25 min). On a normal day this is fine because each long-running job finishes in 1-5 min; but if any one of them hangs on a shared resource (Coolbet session lock waiting on Imperva refresh, AF rate-limit semaphore, or DB pool exhaustion), the others queue up. Once three of four worker threads are occupied, the fourth typically also blocks on the same shared resource — full deadlock. APScheduler doesn't kill long-running jobs; the rows stayed in `pipeline_runs.status='running'` for 22+ minutes until Railway's auto-redeploy reset them.
>
> **Partial fix shipped today** (workers/scheduler.py):
>   1. **Staggered `shadow_interval` to :10/:40** (was :05/:35). Removes the 0-second overlap between betting_refresh + shadow at the same firing minute. Worst-case overlap drops from "simultaneous" to "5 min apart". Doesn't eliminate the shared-resource bug but reduces the chance two long-running siblings collide.
>   2. **Added `EVENT_JOB_MAX_INSTANCES` listener** that logs to console + `_recent_errors` whenever APScheduler skips a fire because the previous instance is still running. On 2026-06-01 this would have produced visible warnings at 14:50, 15:05, 15:20 etc. instead of silent thread-pool exhaustion.
>
> **Still TBD** (would need Railway log access I don't have):
>   • **Identify the actual shared lock** that all three jobs waited on. Strong suspects: Coolbet `CoolbetSession.refresh_jwt_if_needed()` which retries with exponential backoff up to ~10 min; AF `RateLimitedSession` semaphore; DB pool at 20-connection ceiling.
>   • **Per-job timeout**. APScheduler has no built-in per-job timeout. Options: wrap each job body in `concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor.submit(...).result(timeout=…)`, or use a signal-based watchdog (Unix only). Either requires substantial refactor; recommend deferring until the shared-lock root cause is confirmed.
>   • **Separate executor for placer-touching jobs** so they can't starve the rest of the pool. E.g. `executors={"default": ThreadPoolExecutor(4), "placer": ThreadPoolExecutor(1)}` and assign `betting_pipeline`, `betting_refresh`, `coolbet_odds_snapshot`, `shadow_interval` to `placer`. Forces them to serialize, freeing the default pool. Worth a follow-up dev/active/ plan if the hang repeats.
>
> Smoke: SCHEDULER-HANG-MITIGATION.
>
> ## 2026-06-01 — bot_high_alignment RETIREMENT-TRIGGER recorded
>
> Explicit decision locked in before context fades. `bot_high_alignment` is the current biggest paper-prematch firehose (n=210+ last 7d) and is bleeding −5% ROI. BUT it's the only bot firing on BTTS / DC / DNB markets (the multi-profile specialists are starved by data availability + tight edge thresholds — see Task A funnel diag above). Therefore: do NOT retire today. CLV is positive +8.9% so the model is finding sharp picks; the calibration miss is what v20260531 promotion is expected to fix.
>
> **Retirement trigger** (operator decision, not automated): retire `bot_high_alignment` if, after the v20260531 promotion (target Sunday 2026-06-07), BOTH conditions hold over a 7-day window starting from the promotion date:
>   • Live ROI < −2% on n ≥ 50 settled bets
>   • Avg CLV < +5% (calibration failed to recover — picks no longer sharp)
>
> **Hold trigger**: if CLV stays ≥ +5% but ROI is still negative, do NOT retire — the picks are still sharp and the variance can flip; wait another 7 days. Only retire when both signals agree.
>
> **Don't-retire-because-coverage trigger** still applies: if `bot_ou_specialist` Over 3.5 Global (now at 10% edge after today's loosen) + a DNB-coverage solution still aren't firing on BTTS/DC/DNB, hold bot_high_alignment regardless — coverage trumps performance until specialists can pick up the slack.

> Re-evaluate this rule on 2026-06-15 after the first post-promotion week. Filed as ⬜ scheduled.
